      let Ke;
      const at = Z + X;
      const Fe = Z - X;
      const Be = Math.sqrt(Fe * Fe + 4 * se * se);
      at > 0 ? (pe = .5 * (at + Be), Ke = 1 / pe, ge = Z * Ke * X - se * Ke * se) : at < 0 ? ge = .5 * (at - Be) : (pe = .5 * Be, ge = -.5 * Be);
      Fe > 0 ? Se = Fe + Be : Se = Fe - Be;
      Math.abs(Se) > 2 * Math.abs(se) ? (Ke = -2 * se / Se, Oe = 1 / Math.sqrt(1 + Ke * Ke), Se = Ke * Oe) : Math.abs(se) === 0 ? (Se = 1, Oe = 0) : (Ke = -.5 * Se / se, Se = 1 / Math.sqrt(1 + Ke * Ke), Oe = Ke * Se);
      Fe > 0 && (Ke = Se, Se = -Oe, Oe = Ke);
      return {
        rt1: pe,
        rt2: ge,
        cs: Se,
        sn: Oe
      };
    }
    const H = [];
    const ee = {};
    const $ = [];
    const te = new Wi();
    const re = new Wi();
    const ne = new Wi();
    const le = new Wi();
    const me = new Gt();
    const Me = new ve();
    const Je = new Wi();
    const ke = new DOMParser().parseFromString(t, "image/svg+xml");
    i(ke.documentElement, {
      fill: "#000",
      fillOpacity: 1,
      strokeOpacity: 1,
      strokeWidth: 1,
      strokeLineJoin: "miter",
      strokeLineCap: "butt",
      strokeMiterLimit: 4
    });
    return {
      paths: H,
      xml: ke.documentElement
    };
  }
  static createShapes(t) {
    const i = {
      ORIGIN: 0,
      DESTINATION: 1,
      BETWEEN: 2,
      LEFT: 3,
      RIGHT: 4,
      BEHIND: 5,
      BEYOND: 6
    };
    const r = {
      loc: i.ORIGIN,
      t: 0
    };
    function s(E, b, w, T) {
      const x = E.x;
      const R = b.x;
      const D = w.x;
      const O = T.x;
      const z = E.y;
      const Q = b.y;
      const G = w.y;
      const N = T.y;
      const F = (O - D) * (z - G) - (N - G) * (x - D);
      const H = (R - x) * (z - G) - (Q - z) * (x - D);
      const ee = (N - G) * (R - x) - (O - D) * (Q - z);
      const $ = F / ee;
      const te = H / ee;
      if (ee === 0 && F !== 0 || $ <= 0 || $ >= 1 || te < 0 || te > 1) {
        return null;
      }
      if (F === 0 && ee === 0) {
        for (let re = 0; re < 2; re++) {
          o(re === 0 ? w : T, E, b);
          if (r.loc == i.ORIGIN) {
            const ne = re === 0 ? w : T;
            return {
              x: ne.x,
              y: ne.y,
              t: r.t
            };
          } else {
            if (r.loc == i.BETWEEN) {
              return {
                x: +(x + r.t * (R - x)).toPrecision(10),
                y: +(z + r.t * (Q - z)).toPrecision(10),
                t: r.t
              };
            }
          }
        }
        return null;
      } else {
        for (let re = 0; re < 2; re++) {
          o(re === 0 ? w : T, E, b);
          if (r.loc == i.ORIGIN) {
            const ne = re === 0 ? w : T;
            return {
              x: ne.x,
              y: ne.y,
              t: r.t
            };
          }
        }
        return {
          x: +(x + $ * (R - x)).toPrecision(10),
          y: +(z + $ * (Q - z)).toPrecision(10),
          t: $
        };
      }
    }
    function o(E, b, w) {
      const T = w.x - b.x;
      const x = w.y - b.y;
      const R = E.x - b.x;
      const D = E.y - b.y;
      const O = T * D - R * x;
      if (E.x === b.x && E.y === b.y) {
        r.loc = i.ORIGIN;
        r.t = 0;
        return;
      }
      if (E.x === w.x && E.y === w.y) {
        r.loc = i.DESTINATION;
        r.t = 1;
        return;
      }
      if (O < -Number.EPSILON) {
        r.loc = i.LEFT;
        return;
      }
      if (O > Number.EPSILON) {
        r.loc = i.RIGHT;
        return;
      }
      if (T * R < 0 || x * D < 0) {
        r.loc = i.BEHIND;
        return;
      }
      if (Math.sqrt(T * T + x * x) < Math.sqrt(R * R + D * D)) {
        r.loc = i.BEYOND;
        return;
      }
      let z;
      T !== 0 ? z = R / T : z = D / x;
      r.loc = i.BETWEEN;
      r.t = z;
    }
    function a(E, b) {
      const w = [];
      const T = [];
      for (let x = 1; x < E.length; x++) {
        const R = E[x - 1];
        const D = E[x];
        for (let O = 1; O < b.length; O++) {
          const z = b[O - 1];
          const Q = b[O];
          const G = s(R, D, z, Q);
          G !== null && w.find(N => N.t <= G.t + Number.EPSILON && N.t >= G.t - Number.EPSILON) === void 0 && (w.push(G), T.push(new Gt(G.x, G.y)));
        }
      }
      return T;
    }
    function l(E, b, w) {
      const T = new Gt();
      b.getCenter(T);
      const x = [];
      w.forEach(R => {
        R.boundingBox.containsPoint(T) && a(E, R.points).forEach(D => {
          x.push({
            identifier: R.identifier,
            isCW: R.isCW,
            point: D
          });
        });
      });
      x.sort((R, D) => R.point.x - D.point.x);
      return x;
    }
    function u(E, b, w, T, x) {
      (x == null || x === "") && (x = "nonzero");
      const R = new Gt();
      E.boundingBox.getCenter(R);
      const D = l([new Gt(w, R.y), new Gt(T, R.y)], E.boundingBox, b);
      D.sort((F, H) => F.point.x - H.point.x);
      const O = [];
      const z = [];
      D.forEach(F => {
        F.identifier === E.identifier ? O.push(F) : z.push(F);
      });
      const Q = O[0].point.x;
      const G = [];
      let N = 0;
      for (; N < z.length && z[N].point.x < Q;) {
        G.length > 0 && G[G.length - 1] === z[N].identifier ? G.pop() : G.push(z[N].identifier);
        N++;
      }
      G.push(E.identifier);
      if (x === "evenodd") {
        const F = G.length % 2 === 0;
        const H = G[G.length - 2];
        return {
          identifier: E.identifier,
          isHole: F,
          for: H
        };
      } else {
        if (x === "nonzero") {
          let F = !0;
          let H = null;
          let ee = null;
          for (let $ = 0; $ < G.length; $++) {
            const te = G[$];
            F ? (ee = b[te].isCW, F = !1, H = te) : ee !== b[te].isCW && (ee = b[te].isCW, F = !0);
          }
          return {
            identifier: E.identifier,
            isHole: F,
            for: H
          };
        } else {
          console.warn('fill-rule: "' + x + '" is currently not implemented.');
        }
      }
    }
    let d = 999999999;
    let h = -999999999;
    let p = t.subPaths.map(E => {
      const b = E.getPoints();
      let w = -999999999;
      let T = 999999999;
      let x = -999999999;
      let R = 999999999;
      for (let D = 0; D < b.length; D++) {
        const O = b[D];
        O.y > w && (w = O.y);
        O.y < T && (T = O.y);
        O.x > x && (x = O.x);
        O.x < R && (R = O.x);
      }
      h <= x && (h = x + 1);
      d >= R && (d = R - 1);
      return {
        curves: E.curves,
        points: b,
        isCW: b0.isClockWise(b),
        identifier: -1,
        boundingBox: new jF(new Gt(R, T), new Gt(x, w))
      };
    });
    p = p.filter(E => E.points.length > 1);
    for (let E = 0; E < p.length; E++) {
      p[E].identifier = E;
    }
    const A = p.map(E => u(E, p, d, h, t.userData ? t.userData.style.fillRule : void 0));
    const y = [];
    p.forEach(E => {
      if (!A[E.identifier].isHole) {
        const b = new qv();
        b.curves = E.curves;
        A.filter(w => w.isHole && w.for === E.identifier).forEach(w => {
          const T = p[w.identifier];
          const x = new Xb();
          x.curves = T.curves;
          b.holes.push(x);
        });
        y.push(b);
      }
    });
    return y;
  }
  static getStrokeStyle(t, n, i, r, s) {
    t = t !== void 0 ? t : 1;
    n = n !== void 0 ? n : "#000";
    i = i !== void 0 ? i : "miter";
    r = r !== void 0 ? r : "butt";
    s = s !== void 0 ? s : 4;
    return {
      strokeColor: n,
      strokeWidth: t,
      strokeLineJoin: i,
      strokeLineCap: r,
      strokeMiterLimit: s
    };
  }
  static pointsToStroke(t, n, i, r) {
    const s = [];
    const o = [];
    const a = [];
    if (are.pointsToStrokeWithBuffers(t, n, i, r, s, o, a) === 0) {
      return null;
    }
    const l = new qi();
    l.setAttribute("position", new ii(s, 3));
    l.setAttribute("normal", new ii(o, 3));
    l.setAttribute("uv", new ii(a, 2));
    return l;
  }
  static pointsToStrokeWithBuffers(t, n, i, r, s, o, a, l) {
    const u = new Gt();
    const d = new Gt();
    const h = new Gt();
    const p = new Gt();
    const A = new Gt();
    const y = new Gt();
    const E = new Gt();
    const b = new Gt();
    const w = new Gt();
    const T = new Gt();
    const x = new Gt();
    const R = new Gt();
    const D = new Gt();
    const O = new Gt();
    const z = new Gt();
    const Q = new Gt();
    const G = new Gt();
    i = i !== void 0 ? i : 12;
    r = r !== void 0 ? r : .001;
    l = l !== void 0 ? l : 0;
    t = Be(t);
    const N = t.length;
    if (N < 2) {
      return 0;
    }
    const F = t[0].equals(t[N - 1]);
    let H;
    let ee = t[0];
    let $;
    const te = n.strokeWidth / 2;
    const re = 1 / (N - 1);
    let ne = 0;
    let le;
    let me;
    let Me;
    let Je;
    let ke = !1;
    let Z = 0;
    let se = l * 3;
    let X = l * 2;
    pe(t[0], t[1], u).multiplyScalar(te);
    b.copy(t[0]).sub(u);
    w.copy(t[0]).add(u);
    T.copy(b);
    x.copy(w);
    for (let De = 1; De < N; De++) {
      H = t[De];
      De === N - 1 ? F ? $ = t[1] : $ = void 0 : $ = t[De + 1];
      const et = u;
      pe(ee, H, et);
      h.copy(et).multiplyScalar(te);
      R.copy(H).sub(h);
      D.copy(H).add(h);
      le = ne + re;
      me = !1;
      if ($ !== void 0) {
        pe(H, $, d);
        h.copy(d).multiplyScalar(te);
        O.copy(H).sub(h);
        z.copy(H).add(h);
        Me = !0;
        h.subVectors($, ee);
        et.dot(h) < 0 && (Me = !1);
        De === 1 && (ke = Me);
        h.subVectors($, H);
        h.normalize();
        const rt = Math.abs(et.dot(h));
        if (rt > Number.EPSILON) {
          const ze = te / rt;
          h.multiplyScalar(-ze);
          p.subVectors(H, ee);
          A.copy(p).setLength(ze).add(h);
          Q.copy(A).negate();
          const Ue = A.length();
          const Ne = p.length();
          p.divideScalar(Ne);
          y.subVectors($, H);
          const Jt = y.length();
          y.divideScalar(Jt);
          p.dot(Q) < Ne && y.dot(Q) < Jt && (me = !0);
          G.copy(A).add(H);
          Q.add(H);
          Je = !1;
          me ? Me ? (z.copy(Q), D.copy(Q)) : (O.copy(Q), R.copy(Q)) : Oe();
          switch (n.strokeLineJoin) {
            case "bevel":
              Ke(Me, me, le);
              break;
            case "round":
              at(Me, me);
              Me ? Se(H, R, O, le, 0) : Se(H, z, D, le, 1);
              break;
            default:
              const Zt = te * n.strokeMiterLimit / Ue;
              if (Zt < 1) {
                if (n.strokeLineJoin !== "miter-clip") {
                  Ke(Me, me, le);
                  break;
                } else {
                  at(Me, me);
                  Me ? (y.subVectors(G, R).multiplyScalar(Zt).add(R), E.subVectors(G, O).multiplyScalar(Zt).add(O), ge(R, le, 0), ge(y, le, 0), ge(H, le, .5), ge(H, le, .5), ge(y, le, 0), ge(E, le, 0), ge(H, le, .5), ge(E, le, 0), ge(O, le, 0)) : (y.subVectors(G, D).multiplyScalar(Zt).add(D), E.subVectors(G, z).multiplyScalar(Zt).add(z), ge(D, le, 1), ge(y, le, 1), ge(H, le, .5), ge(H, le, .5), ge(y, le, 1), ge(E, le, 1), ge(H, le, .5), ge(E, le, 1), ge(z, le, 1));
                }
              } else {
                me ? (Me ? (ge(w, ne, 1), ge(b, ne, 0), ge(G, le, 0), ge(w, ne, 1), ge(G, le, 0), ge(Q, le, 1)) : (ge(w, ne, 1), ge(b, ne, 0), ge(G, le, 1), ge(b, ne, 0), ge(Q, le, 0), ge(G, le, 1)), Me ? O.copy(G) : z.copy(G)) : Me ? (ge(R, le, 0), ge(G, le, 0), ge(H, le, .5), ge(H, le, .5), ge(G, le, 0), ge(O, le, 0)) : (ge(D, le, 1), ge(G, le, 1), ge(H, le, .5), ge(H, le, .5), ge(G, le, 1), ge(z, le, 1));
                Je = !0;
              }
          }
        } else {
          Oe();
        }
      } else {
        Oe();
      }
      !F && De === N - 1 && Fe(t[0], T, x, Me, !0, ne);
      ne = le;
      ee = H;
      b.copy(O);
      w.copy(z);
    }
    if (!F) {
      Fe(H, R, D, Me, !1, le);
    } else {
      if (me && s) {
        let De = G;
        let et = Q;
        ke !== Me && (De = Q, et = G);
        Me ? (Je || ke) && (et.toArray(s, 0), et.toArray(s, 9), Je && De.toArray(s, 3)) : (Je || !ke) && (et.toArray(s, 3), et.toArray(s, 9), Je && De.toArray(s, 0));
      }
    }
    return Z;
    function pe(De, et, rt) {
      rt.subVectors(et, De);
      return rt.set(-rt.y, rt.x).normalize();
    }
    function ge(De, et, rt) {
      s && (s[se] = De.x, s[se + 1] = De.y, s[se + 2] = 0, o && (o[se] = 0, o[se + 1] = 0, o[se + 2] = 1), se += 3, a && (a[X] = et, a[X + 1] = rt, X += 2));
      Z += 3;
    }
    function Se(De, et, rt, ze, Ue) {
      u.copy(et).sub(De).normalize();
      d.copy(rt).sub(De).normalize();
      let Ne = Math.PI;
      const Jt = u.dot(d);
      Math.abs(Jt) < 1 && (Ne = Math.abs(Math.acos(Jt)));
      Ne /= i;
      h.copy(et);
      for (let Zt = 0, hn = i - 1; Zt < hn; Zt++) {
        p.copy(h).rotateAround(De, Ne);
        ge(h, ze, Ue);
        ge(p, ze, Ue);
        ge(De, ze, .5);
        h.copy(p);
      }
      ge(h, ze, Ue);
      ge(rt, ze, Ue);
      ge(De, ze, .5);
    }
    function Oe() {
      ge(w, ne, 1);
      ge(b, ne, 0);
      ge(R, le, 0);
      ge(w, ne, 1);
      ge(R, le, 0);
      ge(D, le, 1);
    }
    function Ke(De, et, rt) {
      et ? De ? (ge(w, ne, 1), ge(b, ne, 0), ge(R, le, 0), ge(w, ne, 1), ge(R, le, 0), ge(Q, le, 1), ge(R, rt, 0), ge(O, rt, 0), ge(Q, rt, .5)) : (ge(w, ne, 1), ge(b, ne, 0), ge(D, le, 1), ge(b, ne, 0), ge(Q, le, 0), ge(D, le, 1), ge(D, rt, 1), ge(Q, rt, 0), ge(z, rt, 1)) : De ? (ge(R, rt, 0), ge(O, rt, 0), ge(H, rt, .5)) : (ge(D, rt, 1), ge(z, rt, 0), ge(H, rt, .5));
    }
    function at(De, et) {
      et && (De ? (ge(w, ne, 1), ge(b, ne, 0), ge(R, le, 0), ge(w, ne, 1), ge(R, le, 0), ge(Q, le, 1), ge(R, ne, 0), ge(H, le, .5), ge(Q, le, 1), ge(H, le, .5), ge(O, ne, 0), ge(Q, le, 1)) : (ge(w, ne, 1), ge(b, ne, 0), ge(D, le, 1), ge(b, ne, 0), ge(Q, le, 0), ge(D, le, 1), ge(D, ne, 1), ge(Q, le, 0), ge(H, le, .5), ge(H, le, .5), ge(Q, le, 0), ge(z, ne, 1)));
    }
    function Fe(De, et, rt, ze, Ue, Ne) {
      switch (n.strokeLineCap) {
        case "round":
          Ue ? Se(De, rt, et, Ne, .5) : Se(De, et, rt, Ne, .5);
          break;
        case "square":
          if (Ue) {
            u.subVectors(et, De);
            d.set(u.y, -u.x);
            h.addVectors(u, d).add(De);
            p.subVectors(d, u).add(De);
            ze ? (h.toArray(s, 3), p.toArray(s, 0), p.toArray(s, 9)) : (h.toArray(s, 3), a[7] === 1 ? p.toArray(s, 9) : h.toArray(s, 9), p.toArray(s, 0));
          } else {
            u.subVectors(rt, De);
            d.set(u.y, -u.x);
            h.addVectors(u, d).add(De);
            p.subVectors(d, u).add(De);
            const Jt = s.length;
            ze ? (h.toArray(s, Jt - 3), p.toArray(s, Jt - 6), p.toArray(s, Jt - 12)) : (p.toArray(s, Jt - 6), h.toArray(s, Jt - 3), p.toArray(s, Jt - 12));
          }
      }
    }
    function Be(De) {
      let et = !1;
      for (let ze = 1, Ue = De.length - 1; ze < Ue; ze++) {
        if (De[ze].distanceTo(De[ze + 1]) < r) {
          et = !0;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (!et) {
        return De;
      }
      const rt = [];
      rt.push(De[0]);
      for (let ze = 1, Ue = De.length - 1; ze < Ue; ze++) {
        De[ze].distanceTo(De[ze + 1]) >= r && rt.push(De[ze]);
      }
      rt.push(De[De.length - 1]);
      return rt;
    }
  }
};
var aDe = class lre extends sDe {
  inputConfig;
  constructor(t, n, i) {
    const r = bs(void 0);
    super(t, n, {
      ...i,
      remeasureOnChildrenChange: !1,
      depthWriteDefault: !1,
      supportFillProperty: !0,
      boundingBox: r
    });
    this.inputConfig = i;
    const s = bs(void 0);
    Xie(s, lDe, cDe, this.abortSignal, Er(() => ({
      src: this.properties.value.src,
      content: this.properties.value.content
    })));
    ji(() => {
      const o = s.value;
      r.value = o?.boundingBox;
      if (o == null || o.meshes.length === 0) {
        this.notifyAncestorsChanged();
        return;
      }
      super.addUnsafe(...o.meshes);
      this.notifyAncestorsChanged();
      return () => {
        super.remove(...o.meshes);
      };
    }, this.abortSignal);
  }
  add() {
    throw new Error("the svg component can not have any children");
  }
  clone(t) {
    const n = new lre(this.inputProperties, this.initialClasses, this.inputConfig);
    this.copyInto(n, t);
    return n;
  }
};
var nj = new Map();
var ij = new ore();
async function lDe({
  src: e,
  content: t
}) {
  if (e == null && t == null) {
    return;
  }
  let n;
  if (e != null) {
    let o = nj.get(e);
    o == null && nj.set(e, o = ij.loadAsync(e));
    n = await o;
  } else {
    n = ij.parse(t);
  }
  const i = [];
  for (const o of n.paths) {
    const a = ore.createShapes(o);
    const l = new ql({
      color: o.color,
      toneMapped: !1
    });
    for (const u of a) {
      const d = new nr(new LT(u), l);
      d.matrixAutoUpdate = !1;
      d.scale.y = -1;
      d.updateMatrix();
      i.push(d);
    }
  }
  let r;
  const s = n.xml.getAttribute("viewBox")?.split(/\s+/).map(o => Number.parseFloat(o)).filter(o => !isNaN(o));
  if (s?.length === 4) {
    const [o, a, l, u] = s;
    r = {
      center: new ve(l / 2 + o, -u / 2 - a, 0),
      size: new ve(l, u, 1e-5)
    };
  }
  return {
    meshes: i,
    boundingBox: r
  };
}
function cDe(e) {
  e?.meshes.forEach(t => {
    t.material instanceof tu && t.material.dispose();
    t.geometry.dispose();
  });
}
function S9(e, t = e.name) {
  e9({
    [`Vanilla${t}`]: e
  });
  return (0, de.forwardRef)(({
    children: n,
    ...i
  }, r) => {
    const s = (0, de.useRef)(null);
    const o = (0, de.useRef)(i);
    o.current = i;
    (0, de.useImperativeHandle)(r, () => s.current, []);
    const a = cre();
    const l = ure(s, i, (0, de.useMemo)(() => [o.current, void 0, {
      renderContext: a
    }], [a]));
    const Component = `vanilla${t}`;
    return <Component ref={s} {...l}>{n}</Component>;
  });
}
function cre() {
  const e = il(t => t.invalidate);
  return (0, de.useMemo)(() => ({
    requestFrame: e
  }), [e]);
